## Cabo Verde added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cabo Verde’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 books, all of them student (pupil) manuals of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde: *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (Edição 2019), *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (Edição 2020) and *Matemática 7º ano — Manual do(a) aluno(a)* (Edição 2020); 0 teacher guides** — and placed 197 teaching items (Grade 3: 47, Grade 5: 69, Grade 7: 81; 391 PDF pages, every page read). The expansion map’s count of 3 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT. Set boundary, proved from the records: `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly 17 Cabo Verde rows (all Government, grades 1–7), of which exactly three are Mathematics, all student; the download list’s §C enumerates 93 ministry manuals, including Maths manuals for grades 1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 9, 10 and 11 and applied-Maths manuals — every one of those is exact-link-only (no TSV row, no staging file, no GitHub copy) and NOT in hand, so none is cited here; counting any of them would be a set error. Record gap, reported not fixed (record-side): `phase3-push-state.json` contains no Cabo Verde entry at all, and there is no Cabo Verde folder or `books.json` in phase3-staging — the in-hand evidence is the TSV’s GitHub URLs themselves, and all three GitHub copies were located, verified (byte size and MD5; the Grade 5 ministry copy is byte-identical to the GitHub copy, so that book is one book, cited once) and read directly, so the set is settled despite the push-state gap. No duplicates exist in the set: three distinct grades, three distinct books. Printed page = PDF page in all three books. The placements’ named exclusions — front matter, unit openers, objectives lists and the closing national-anthem page in the Grade 5 and Grade 7 books; the Grade 3 book’s cover plate and welcome/mascot pages (PDF pp. 1, 3, 5), its part-opener pages (PDF pp. 55, 85, 107, 111), its closing test “Teste” (printed pp. 125–129 — mixed assessment re-using taught content, no new teaching) and the flag / national-anthem page (PDF p. 130) — and pure exercise / consolidation blocks that teach nothing new are stocked nowhere. The 12 book errors found in the read (2 in the Grade 5 book, 5 in the Grade 7 book, 5 in the Grade 3 book) are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items from Book 1.

Printed page = PDF page. Pupil textbook.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items (items 117 and 142).

**Book 1:** *Números, 6º — Folleto de prácticas* — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 6; 192,462 bytes, 48 PDF pp.; a compilation of the practices of the Números texto (its own p. 2). Pages cited by PDF page. Pupil practice book.

**Book 2:** *Números, 6º* (texto) — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 6; 2.ª ed. 2023, ISBN 978-9930-617-29-8; 29,534,557 bytes, 184 PDF pp. The book prints no folio — pages are cited by PDF page with the book's own section numbers (§). Pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Practice set — natural numbers as powers: perfect squares and cubes, expanded (developed) notation with powers of ten (Cap. 2) | Costa Rica — EVEPRIM 6 *Números — Folleto de prácticas* (ITCR) (student practice folleto) — PDF pp. 12–19 |
| Writing and reading numbers in expanded (developed) form with powers of ten (§2.4) | Costa Rica — EVEPRIM 6 *Números* (texto), 2.ª ed. 2023 (ITCR) (student book) — PDF pp. 41–46 |

## Cuba added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cuba's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **29 in-hand entries = 25 distinct works (MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, Cuba), every one read in full**: student textbooks for Grades 1 and 3–12, student workbooks for Grades 1, 2 and 3, and teacher documents (Programa and Orientaciones metodológicas) for Grades 1–5, 8 and 11 — and placed 247 teaching items (202 student-side, 45 teacher-evidence). **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 14 student + 8 teacher is WRONG** — the staging books.json holds 13 student + 9 teacher entries and textbooks.tsv holds 7 further secured entries; duplicated filings are cited once (the Grade 3 and Grade 8 student books were each verified in a second edition; the Grade 4 and Grade 5 student books are each filed twice as byte-identical copies — Grade 4 MD5 01c106741b186cf28d360854a40d7e93, Grade 5 MD5 b48e28b1df820e2451bb44eb33b5ac11). Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Teacher items are TEACHER EVIDENCE — they state what the programme requires and how it is taught — are labelled as such on every row, and are never counted in this lesson's acceptance figure; student textbook and workbook items are COUNTED. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items (items 5, 18, 35, 61, 64, 75, 78, 87, 94): 4 student items and 5 teacher-evidence items.

## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items (items 23, 70, 97).

FULL BOOK (128 lessons, contents to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices). Pupil textbook.

FULL BOOK (112 lessons, contents to printed p. 140). Pupil textbook.

## Djibouti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Djibouti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the CRIPEN *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher's guide, series « À MOI LES MATHS », édition 2023; 129 pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 68 teaching items. The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.
